  (¬∃ x, (∀ (r : N.R), 0 < ρ r x) ∧ ∑ r, ρ r x • N.reactionVector r = 0) ∧

CRNT.birch.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι] (S : Submodule ℝ (ι → ℝ)) {xstar c : ι → ℝ} (hxs : ∀ (i : ι), 0 < xstar i)
  (hc : ∀ (i : ι), 0 < c i) :
  ∃ x,
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < x i) ∧
      x - c ∈ S ∧
        (∀ s ∈ S, ∑ i, (Real.log (x i) - Real.log (xstar i)) * s i = 0) ∧
          ∀ (y : ι → ℝ),
            (∀ (i : ι), 0 < y i) → y - c ∈ S → (∀ s ∈ S, ∑ i, (Real.log (y i) - Real.log (xstar i)) * s i = 0) → y = x

  (κ : N.RateConstants) {n : ℕ} (a b : Fin n → CRNT.Complex S) (c : Fin n → ℝ) (hc : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 < c i)
  (htor :
    ∀ (x : CRNT.Concentration S),
      x.Positive →
        (N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x ↔ ∀ (i : Fin n), (a i).massActionMonomial x = c i * (b i).massActionMonomial x))

  {a : ι → ι → ℝ} {e : ι → ℝ} (ha : ∀ (i j : ι), 0 ≤ a i j) (he : ∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ e i)
  (hdr : CRNT.Elimination.Drained a e) {κ : Type u_2} {X : Type u_3} [Fintype κ] (β : ι → κ → ℝ)

CRNT.Network.messi_no_exactly_two {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)

  (κ : N.RateConstants) {n : ℕ} (a b : Fin n → CRNT.Complex S) (c : Fin n → ℝ) (hc : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 < c i)

    ∀ (x : CRNT.Concentration S),

        (N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x ↔ ∀ (i : Fin n), (a i).massActionMonomial x = c i * (b i).massActionMonomial x))

      x.Positive →
        (N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x ↔ ∀ (i : Fin n), (a i).massActionMonomial x = c i * (b i).massActionMonomial x))
  {xs : CRNT.Concentration S} (hxs : xs.Positive) (hxss : N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ xs)
  (g : CRNT.Concentration S → ℝ) :
  (∀ (x y : CRNT.Concentration S),
      x.Positive → N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x → y.Positive → N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ y → g x = g y) ↔
    ∀ v ∈ CRNT.expLogKernel a b, g (CRNT.toricParametrization xs v) = g xs

CRNT.Network.sys_augCoeff {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] {E₁ E₂ : Type} [Fintype E₁] [Fintype E₂]
  (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) (Z : (S → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] E₁ → ℝ) (L : (S → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] E₂ → ℝ) (T : E₂ → ℝ)
  (h : CRNT.Complex S → ℤ) (hh : ∀ (i : S), h (CRNT.Network.unitComplex i) = 0) (t : ℝ) (x : S → ℝ) :
  CRNT.Patchworking.sys N.augSupport (N.augCoeff κ Z L T) h t x =
    Sum.elim (Z (N.massActionVectorField (N.deformedRatesZ κ h t) x)) (L x - Real.exp (↑(h 0) * Real.log t) • T)

CRNT.Network.fullyOpen_massActionVectorField_eq {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.fullyOpen.RateConstants) (x : CRNT.Concentration S) (s : S) :
  N.fullyOpen.massActionVectorField κ x s =
    ∑ r, κ.k (Sum.inl r) * (N.reaction r).source.massActionMonomial x * N.reactionVector r s +
        κ.k (Sum.inr (Sum.inl s)) -
      κ.k (Sum.inr (Sum.inr s)) * x s

CRNT.Network.massActionJacobian_eq_factorization {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) {x : CRNT.Concentration S} (hx : x.Positive) :
  N.massActionJacobian κ x =
    N.complexSpeciesMatrix.transpose *
      (N.weightedKineticLaplacian κ x * (N.complexSpeciesMatrix * Matrix.diagonal fun s => 1 / x s))

CRNT.Network.massActionVectorField_eq_transfer {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (h : N.Unimolecular) (x : CRNT.Concentration S) (s : S) :
  N.massActionVectorField κ x s =
    (∑ r, if N.uniTarget h r = s then κ.k r * x (N.uniSource h r) else 0) -
      ∑ r, if N.uniSource h r = s then κ.k r * x s else 0

  CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x ↔
    κ.k CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.fwd * x CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.X =
      κ.k CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.bwd * x CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.Y

  CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ
      (CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.N.kingAltmanSteadyState κ CRNT.Examples.KingAltman.N_unimolecular) ∧

CRNT.Network.multistationarity_inherited_union {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N M : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (κM : M.RateConstants) (hS : N.stoichSubspace = ⊤) {x₁ x₂ : CRNT.Concentration S}
  (hx₁ : x₁.Positive) (hx₂ : x₂.Positive) (hne : x₁ ≠ x₂) (hss₁ : N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x₁)
  (hss₂ : N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x₂) (hnd₁ : (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) x₁).IsInvertible)
  (hnd₂ : (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) x₂).IsInvertible) :
  ∃ ε₀,
    0 < ε₀ ∧
      ∀ (ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε),
        ε < ε₀ →
          ∃ y₁ y₂,
            y₁.Positive ∧
              y₂.Positive ∧
                y₁ ≠ y₂ ∧
                  y₁ - y₂ ∈ (N.union M).stoichSubspace ∧
                    (N.union M).IsMassActionSteadyState (CRNT.Network.unionRates κ κM ε hε) y₁ ∧
                      (N.union M).IsMassActionSteadyState (CRNT.Network.unionRates κ κM ε hε) y₂

CRNT.Network.oneRxn_jacobian_apply {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (a b : CRNT.Complex S)
  (κ : (CRNT.Network.oneRxn a b).fullyOpen.RateConstants) (x u : CRNT.Concentration S) (s : S) :
  ((CRNT.Network.oneRxn a b).fullyOpen.massActionJacobianCLM κ x) u s =
    (κ.k (Sum.inl ()) * ∑ j, CRNT.massActionMonomialGrad a x j * u j) * (↑(b s) - ↑(a s)) -
      κ.k (Sum.inr (Sum.inr s)) * u s

CRNT.Network.exists_nondegenerate_branches {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N M : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (κM : M.RateConstants) {x z : CRNT.Concentration S} (hx : x.Positive) (hz : z.Positive)
  (hne : x ≠ z) (hsx : N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ x) (hsz : N.IsMassActionSteadyState κ z)
  (hnx : (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) x).IsInvertible)
  (hnz : (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) z).IsInvertible) :
  ∃ ε,
    0 < ε ∧
      ∃ y₁ y₂,
        y₁.Positive ∧
          y₂.Positive ∧
            y₁ ≠ y₂ ∧
              N.perturbedField M κ κM (ε, y₁) = 0 ∧
                N.perturbedField M κ κM (ε, y₂) = 0 ∧
                  (fderiv ℝ (fun y => N.perturbedField M κ κM (ε, y)) y₁).IsInvertible ∧
                    (fderiv ℝ (fun y => N.perturbedField M κ κM (ε, y)) y₂).IsInvertible

CRNT.Network.reducedDerivOf_pos_iff_two_reaction_circuit {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (v : S → ℝ) (hv : v ≠ 0) (hspan : N.stoichSubspace = ℝ ∙ v) (x : CRNT.Concentration S) (w : N.R → ℝ) {rpos rneg : N.R}
  (hr : rpos ≠ rneg) (hothers : ∀ (r : N.R), r ≠ rpos → r ≠ rneg → N.reducedContribution v hv hspan x r = 0) :

      w rneg *
        (-N.reducedCoefficient v hv hspan rneg * ∑ j, CRNT.massActionMonomialGrad (N.reaction rneg).source x j * v j)

  (A : ι → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) R) (κ : ι → R) :

Matrix.det_mul_diag_mul_eq_sum_subsets.{u_2} {m n : ℕ} {R : Type u_2} [CommRing R] (U : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin n) R)
  (W : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin m) R) (κ : Fin n → R) :
  (U * (Matrix.diagonal κ * W)).det =
    ∑ S ∈ (Finset.powersetCard m Finset.univ).attach,
      ((U.submatrix id ⇑((↑S).orderEmbOfFin ⋯)).det * ∏ i, κ (((↑S).orderEmbOfFin ⋯) i)) *
        (W.submatrix (⇑((↑S).orderEmbOfFin ⋯)) id).det

CRNT.Network.relEntropy_expDecay_along_solution {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (hwr : N.WeaklyReversible) {xstar : CRNT.Concentration S} (hxs : xstar.Positive)
  (hcb : N.IsComplexBalanced κ xstar) :
  ∃ K α ε,
    0 < K ∧
      0 < α ∧
        0 < ε ∧
          ∀ (x₀ : CRNT.Concentration S) (γ : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S),
            x₀.Positive →
              (∀ (s : S), x₀ s ≤ 2 * xstar s) →
                N.StoichCompatible xstar x₀ →
                  ‖x₀ - xstar‖ < ε →
                    γ 0 = x₀ →

                        ∀ (t : ℝ),
                          0 ≤ t → CRNT.relEntropy xstar (γ t) ≤ K * Real.exp (-2 * α * t) * CRNT.relEntropy xstar x₀

                0 < η ∧
                  (∀ (i : ι), CRNT.Persistence.fdot (nrm i) x₀ ≤ c i) ∧

                        (∀ (i : ι), CRNT.Persistence.fdot (nrm i) x ≤ c i) → ∀ (q : Fin 2), η ≤ x q ∧ x q ≤ B) ∧
                      (∀ (σ : ℝ),
                          0 ≤ σ →
                            σ ≤ R' →
                              ∀ (x : Fin 2 → ℝ),

                                  ∀ (i : ι),
                                    CRNT.Persistence.fdot (nrm i) x = c i + σ →
                                      ∀ h ∈ H, 0 < CRNT.Persistence.fdot (nrm i) h → δ ≤ ∑ q, h q * Real.log (x q)

CRNT.Persistence.exists_zsh_family {n : ℕ} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (H : Finset (Fin n → ℝ))
  (hsym : ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ (ρ : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)), h ∘ ⇑ρ ∈ H) :
  ∃ ι x,
    ∃ (_ : Nonempty ι),
      ∃ a bf,
        (∀ (i : ι), MonotoneOn (fun ε => bf ε i) (Set.Ioi 0)) ∧
          (∀ (i : ι), ContinuousOn (fun ε => bf ε i) (Set.Ioi 0)) ∧
            (∀ (i : ι) (x : Fin n → ℝ), (∀ (q : Fin n), 0 < x q) → 0 < CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x) ∧
              ∀ (δ M : ℝ),
                1 ≤ M →
                  ∃ ε₁,
                    0 < ε₁ ∧
                      ∀ (ε : ℝ),
                        0 < ε →
                          ε < ε₁ →
                            (∀ (μ : ℝ),
                                0 < μ →
                                  (∀ m < n, ε ^ CRNT.Persistence.eexp H m < μ / 2) →
                                    ∀ (x₀ : Fin n → ℝ),
                                      (∀ (q : Fin n), μ ≤ x₀ q) → ∀ (i : ι), bf ε i ≤ CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x₀) ∧
                              (∀ (x : Fin n → ℝ),
                                  (∀ (i : ι), bf ε i ≤ CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x) →
                                    ∀ (q : Fin n), ε ^ CRNT.Persistence.eexp H 0 ≤ x q) ∧
                                (∀ (x : Fin n → ℝ),
                                    (∀ (i : ι),
                                        bf ε i - ε ^ CRNT.Persistence.eexp H 0 / 2 ≤ CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x) →
                                      ∀ (q : Fin n), ε ^ CRNT.Persistence.eexp H 0 / 2 ≤ x q) ∧
                                  ∀ (σ : ℝ),
                                    0 ≤ σ →
                                      σ ≤ ε ^ CRNT.Persistence.eexp H 0 / 2 →
                                        ∀ (x : Fin n → ℝ),
                                          (∀ (q : Fin n), 0 < x q) →
                                            (∀ (q : Fin n), x q ≤ M) →
                                              (∀ (i : ι), bf ε i - σ ≤ CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x) →
                                                ∀ (i : ι),
                                                  CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) x = bf ε i - σ →
                                                    ∀ h ∈ H,
                                                      CRNT.Persistence.fdot (a i) h < 0 → δ ≤ ∑ q, h q * Real.log (x q)
'CRNT.Persistence.exists_zsh_family' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Persistence.exists_planar_infinity (H : Finset (Fin 2 → ℝ))
  (hsym : ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ (ρ : Equiv.Perm (Fin 2)), h ∘ ⇑ρ ∈ H) :
  ∃ m β,
    2 ≤ m ∧
      β (m - 1) = 1 ∧
        (∀ (k : ℕ), k + 1 < m → β k < β (k + 1)) ∧
          (∀ k < m, |β k| ≤ |β 0| + 1) ∧
            (∀ (k : ℕ), k + 1 < m → (β k + β (k + 1)) / 2 < 1) ∧
              ∀ (δ : ℝ),
                ∃ C₀,
                  4 ≤ C₀ ∧
                    ∀ (C : ℝ),
                      C₀ ≤ C →
                        (∀ k < m, 0 ≤ CRNT.Persistence.offs β C k ∧ CRNT.Persistence.offs β C k ≤ C / 4) ∧
                          ∀ (σ : ℝ),
                            0 ≤ σ →
                              σ ≤ 1 →
                                ∀ (u v : ℝ),
                                  0 < u →
                                    0 < v →
                                      (∀ k < m, u + β k * v ≤ C + CRNT.Persistence.offs β C k + σ) →
                                        (∀ k < m, β k * u + v ≤ C + CRNT.Persistence.offs β C k + σ) →
                                          ∀ k < m,
                                            u + β k * v = C + CRNT.Persistence.offs β C k + σ →
                                              ∀ h ∈ H, 0 < h 0 + β k * h 1 → δ ≤ h 0 * Real.log u + h 1 * Real.log v
'CRNT.Persistence.exists_planar_infinity' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (hk1 : k < 1) (hc : 0 < c) (hJ : ∀ x ∈ K, ‖N.massActionJacobianCLM κ x‖ ≤ k)
  (hone : ∀ x ∈ K, ∀ (u : CRNT.Concentration S), ∑ s, u s * (N.massActionJacobianCLM κ x) u s ≤ -c * ∑ s, u s ^ 2)

CRNT.hurwitz_cubic_root_iff (z₁ z₂ z₃ : ℂ) (a₂ a₁ a₀ : ℝ) (e₂ : ↑a₂ = -(z₁ + z₂ + z₃))
  (e₁ : ↑a₁ = z₁ * z₂ + z₁ * z₃ + z₂ * z₃) (e₀ : ↑a₀ = -(z₁ * z₂ * z₃))
  (hstruct : z₁.im = 0 ∧ z₂.im = 0 ∧ z₃.im = 0 ∨ z₃ = (starRingEnd ℂ) z₂) :
  0 < a₂ ∧ 0 < a₁ ∧ 0 < a₀ ∧ a₀ < a₂ * a₁ ↔ z₁.re < 0 ∧ z₂.re < 0 ∧ z₃.re < 0

CRNT.hurwitz_quartic_root_iff (z₁ z₂ z₃ z₄ : ℂ) (a₃ a₂ a₁ a₀ : ℝ) (e₃ : ↑a₃ = -(z₁ + z₂ + z₃ + z₄))
  (e₂ : ↑a₂ = z₁ * z₂ + z₁ * z₃ + z₁ * z₄ + z₂ * z₃ + z₂ * z₄ + z₃ * z₄)
  (e₁ : ↑a₁ = -(z₁ * z₂ * z₃ + z₁ * z₂ * z₄ + z₁ * z₃ * z₄ + z₂ * z₃ * z₄)) (e₀ : ↑a₀ = z₁ * z₂ * z₃ * z₄)
  (hstruct :
    z₁.im = 0 ∧ z₂.im = 0 ∧ z₃.im = 0 ∧ z₄.im = 0 ∨
      z₂ = (starRingEnd ℂ) z₁ ∧ z₃.im = 0 ∧ z₄.im = 0 ∨ z₂ = (starRingEnd ℂ) z₁ ∧ z₄ = (starRingEnd ℂ) z₃) :
  0 < a₃ ∧ 0 < a₂ ∧ 0 < a₁ ∧ 0 < a₀ ∧ a₁ ^ 2 + a₃ ^ 2 * a₀ < a₃ * a₂ * a₁ ↔
    z₁.re < 0 ∧ z₂.re < 0 ∧ z₃.re < 0 ∧ z₄.re < 0

CRNT.Hopf.hopf_cubic_resonance {ω : ℝ} (_hω : ω ≠ 0) :
  2 * (↑ω * Complex.I) + CRNT.Hopf.cconj (↑ω * Complex.I) = ↑ω * Complex.I

CRNT.Hopf.quadForm_expansion (q20 q11 q02 : ℂ) (u v : ℝ) :
  CRNT.Hopf.quadForm q20 q11 q02 (↑u + ↑v * Complex.I) =
    ↑((q20 + q11 + q02).re * u ^ 2 - 2 * (q20 - q02).im * u * v - (q20 - q11 + q02).re * v ^ 2) +
      Complex.I * (↑(q20 + q11 + q02).im * ↑u ^ 2 + 2 * ↑(q20 - q02).re * ↑u * ↑v - ↑(q20 - q11 + q02).im * ↑v ^ 2)
'CRNT.Hopf.quadForm_expansion' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.cubicForm_expansion (c30 c21 c12 c03 : ℂ) (u v : ℝ) :
  CRNT.Hopf.cubicForm c30 c21 c12 c03 (↑u + ↑v * Complex.I) =
    ↑((c30 + c21 + c12 + c03).re * u ^ 3 - (3 * c30 + c21 - c12 - 3 * c03).im * u ^ 2 * v +
            (-3 * c30 + c21 + c12 - 3 * c03).re * u * v ^ 2 -
          (-c30 + c21 - c12 + c03).im * v ^ 3) +
      Complex.I *
        (↑(c30 + c21 + c12 + c03).im * ↑u ^ 3 + ↑(3 * c30 + c21 - c12 - 3 * c03).re * ↑u ^ 2 * ↑v +
            ↑(-3 * c30 + c21 + c12 - 3 * c03).im * ↑u * ↑v ^ 2 +
          ↑(-c30 + c21 - c12 + c03).re * ↑v ^ 3)
'CRNT.Hopf.cubicForm_expansion' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.cubicCombination_eq (q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03 : ℂ) :
  (CRNT.Hopf.planarPartialsOf q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03).fxxx +
          (CRNT.Hopf.planarPartialsOf q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03).fxyy +
        (CRNT.Hopf.planarPartialsOf q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03).gxxy +
      (CRNT.Hopf.planarPartialsOf q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03).gyyy =
    16 * c21.re
'CRNT.Hopf.cubicCombination_eq' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.classical_lyapunov_formula {ω : ℝ} (hω : ω ≠ 0) (P : CRNT.Hopf.PlanarPartials) :
  (CRNT.Hopf.resonantCoefficientSol (↑ω * Complex.I) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ20 P) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ11 P)
        (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ02 P) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorC21 P)).re =
    1 / 16 * (P.fxxx + P.fxyy + P.gxxy + P.gyyy) +
      1 / (16 * ω) * (P.fxy * (P.fxx + P.fyy) - P.gxy * (P.gxx + P.gyy) - P.fxx * P.gxx + P.fyy * P.gyy)
'CRNT.Hopf.classical_lyapunov_formula' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.classical_lyapunov {ω : ℝ} (hω : ω ≠ 0) (P : CRNT.Hopf.PlanarPartials) (H : CRNT.PlanarHopfData)
  (hωH : H.ω = ω)
  (hH :
    H.c₁ =
      CRNT.Hopf.resonantCoefficientSol (↑ω * Complex.I) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ20 P) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ11 P)
        (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorQ02 P) (CRNT.Hopf.realTaylorC21 P)) :
  H.lyapunovFromPartials P.fxxx P.fxyy P.gxxy P.gyyy P.fxy P.fxx P.fyy P.gxy P.gxx P.gyy = H.firstLyapunov
'CRNT.Hopf.classical_lyapunov' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.hopf_lyapunov_calibration {ω : ℝ} (hω : ω ≠ 0) (q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03 : ℂ) (H : CRNT.PlanarHopfData)
  (hH : H.c₁ = CRNT.Hopf.resonantCoefficientSol (↑ω * Complex.I) q20 q11 q02 c21) :
  have P := CRNT.Hopf.normalFormPartials (↑ω * Complex.I) q20 q11 q02 c30 c21 c12 c03;
  H.lyapunovFromPartials P.fxxx P.fxyy P.gxxy P.gyyy P.fxy P.fxx P.fyy P.gxy P.gxx P.gyy = H.firstLyapunov
'CRNT.Hopf.hopf_lyapunov_calibration' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Hopf.MassActionHopf.calibration {N : CRNT.Network (Fin 2)} {κ : ℝ → N.RateConstants}
  {xst : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration (Fin 2)} {μ₀ : ℝ} (h : CRNT.Hopf.MassActionHopf N κ xst μ₀) :
  h.H.lyapunovFromPartials h.partials.fxxx h.partials.fxyy h.partials.gxxy h.partials.gyyy h.partials.fxy h.partials.fxx
      h.partials.fyy h.partials.gxy h.partials.gxx h.partials.gyy =
    h.H.firstLyapunov

CRNT.Hopf.MassActionHopf.massAction_hasPeriodicOrbit_of_hopf {N : CRNT.Network (Fin 2)} {κ : ℝ → N.RateConstants}
  {xst : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration (Fin 2)} {μ₀ : ℝ} (h : CRNT.Hopf.MassActionHopf N κ xst μ₀) (branch : Set ℝ)
  (hclosure : h.H.μ₀ ∈ closure branch) (hbranch : ∀ ν ∈ branch, h.H.α ν / h.H.firstLyapunov < 0)
  (hfreq : ∀ ν ∈ branch, h.H.cycleFreq √(-h.H.α ν / h.H.firstLyapunov) ≠ 0) :
  h.H.μ₀ ∈ closure branch ∧
    ∀ μ ∈ branch,
      0 < |2 * Real.pi / h.H.cycleFreq √(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)| ∧
        Function.Periodic (h.H.hopfLimitCycle (√(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)) 0)
            |2 * Real.pi / h.H.cycleFreq √(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)| ∧
          (∃ s t,
              h.H.hopfLimitCycle (√(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)) 0 s ≠
                h.H.hopfLimitCycle (√(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)) 0 t) ∧
            ∃ s, ‖h.H.hopfLimitCycle (√(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)) 0 s‖ = √(-h.H.α μ / h.H.firstLyapunov)
'CRNT.Hopf.MassActionHopf.massAction_hasPeriodicOrbit_of_hopf' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,

  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hmaps : Set.MapsTo f (Set.Icc a b) (Set.Icc a b)) (hmono : Monotone f) {x : ℝ}

CRNT.Oscillation.antitoneIntervalMap_evenOdd_converge {a b : ℝ} (hab : a ≤ b) {f : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hmaps : Set.MapsTo f (Set.Icc a b) (Set.Icc a b))
  (hanti : AntitoneOn f (Set.Icc a b)) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc a b) :
  ∃ p q,
    p ∈ Set.Icc a b ∧
      q ∈ Set.Icc a b ∧
        f p = q ∧
          f q = p ∧
            Filter.Tendsto (fun k => f^[2 * k] x) Filter.atTop (nhds p) ∧
              Filter.Tendsto (fun k => f^[2 * k + 1] x) Filter.atTop (nhds q)
'CRNT.Oscillation.antitoneIntervalMap_evenOdd_converge' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Oscillation.monotoneIntervalMap_orbit_converges_or_twoCycle {a b : ℝ} (hab : a ≤ b) {f : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hmaps : Set.MapsTo f (Set.Icc a b) (Set.Icc a b))
  (hmono : Monotone f ∨ Antitone f) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc a b) :
  (∃ p ∈ Set.Icc a b, f p = p ∧ Filter.Tendsto (fun n => f^[n] x) Filter.atTop (nhds p)) ∨
    ∃ p q,
      p ∈ Set.Icc a b ∧
        q ∈ Set.Icc a b ∧
          f p = q ∧
            f q = p ∧
              p ≠ q ∧
                Filter.Tendsto (fun k => f^[2 * k] x) Filter.atTop (nhds p) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto (fun k => f^[2 * k + 1] x) Filter.atTop (nhds q)
'CRNT.Oscillation.monotoneIntervalMap_orbit_converges_or_twoCycle' depends on axioms: [propext,

  (hsnn : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → 0 ≤ s t) (hdrift : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → |-k₁ * (e₀ - c t) * s t + km₁ * c t| ≤ D) (t : ℝ) :
  0 ≤ t →
    |c t - CRNT.Enzyme.qss k₁ km₁ k₂ e₀ (s t)| ≤
      Real.exp (-(km₁ + k₂) * t) * |c 0 - CRNT.Enzyme.qss k₁ km₁ k₂ e₀ (s 0)| + e₀ / ((km₁ + k₂) / k₁) * D / (km₁ + k₂)

CRNT.Stochastic.complexBalanced_geometric_ergodic {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) {c : CRNT.Concentration S} (hc : c.Positive) (hcb : N.IsComplexBalanced κ c) (V : (S → ℕ) → ℝ)
  (hV0 : ∀ (n : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ V n) (hfinV : ∀ (R : ℝ), {n | V n ≤ R}.Finite) {γ K : ℝ} (hγ : 0 < γ) (hK : 0 ≤ K)
  (hdrift : ∀ (n : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem κ).drift V n ≤ -γ * V n + K)
  (hirr : ∀ (a b : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos a b) :
  (∀ (t : ℝ),
      0 ≤ t →
        ∀ (m : S → ℕ),
          ∑' (n : S → ℕ), CRNT.Network.productPoissonPMF c n * N.transition κ t n m =
            CRNT.Network.productPoissonPMF c m) ∧
    (∃ β,
        0 < β ∧
          ∃ ρ,
            0 ≤ ρ ∧
              ρ < 1 ∧
                ∃ C₀,
                  ∀ (a : S → ℕ) (t : ℝ),
                    0 ≤ t →
                      ∑' (b : S → ℕ), |N.transition κ t a b - CRNT.Network.productPoissonPMF c b| * (1 + β * V b) ≤
                        C₀ * (1 + V a) * ρ ^ ⌊t⌋₊) ∧
      ∀ (a : S → ℕ),
        Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (b : S → ℕ), |N.transition κ t a b - CRNT.Network.productPoissonPMF c b|)
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

CRNT.Network.classProductForm_facts {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  (hWR : N.WeaklyReversible) {c : CRNT.Concentration S} (hc : c.Positive) (hcb : N.IsComplexBalanced κ c) (n : S → ℕ) :
  0 < ∑' (x : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n x ∧
    (∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ N.classProductForm κ c n m / ∑' (x : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n x) ∧
      (∀ (m : S → ℕ),
          ¬(N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n m →
            N.classProductForm κ c n m / ∑' (x : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n x = 0) ∧
        HasSum (fun m => N.classProductForm κ c n m / ∑' (x : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n x) 1 ∧
          ∀ (t : ℝ),
            0 ≤ t →
              ∀ (b : S → ℕ),
                ∑' (x : S → ℕ),
                    (N.classProductForm κ c n x / ∑' (y : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n y) * N.transition κ t x b =
                  N.classProductForm κ c n b / ∑' (y : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n y
'CRNT.Network.classProductForm_facts' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.stationary_eq_classProductForm {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (hWR : N.WeaklyReversible) {c : CRNT.Concentration S} (hc : c.Positive)
  (hcb : N.IsComplexBalanced κ c) (n : S → ℕ) {μ : (S → ℕ) → ℝ} (hμ0 : ∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ μ m)
  (hμE : ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ¬(N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n m → μ m = 0) (hμ1 : HasSum μ 1)
  (hμinv : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), μ x * N.transition κ t x m = μ m) (m : S → ℕ) :
  μ m = N.classProductForm κ c n m / ∑' (x : S → ℕ), N.classProductForm κ c n x

CRNT.Network.foster_lyapunov_ergodic_linear {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (hWR : N.WeaklyReversible) (w : S → ℝ) (hw : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) {c K : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c)
  (hK : 0 ≤ K)
  (hdrift :
    ∀ (n : S → ℕ),
      ∑ r, N.stochasticMassActionRate κ n r * ∑ s, w s * N.reactionVector r s ≤
        -c * CRNT.Network.linearObservable w n + K)

  ∃ π,
    (∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ π m) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), π x * N.transition κ t x m = π m) ∧
          (∀ (m : S → ℕ), ¬(N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n₀ m → π m = 0) ∧
            (∀ (m : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n₀ m → 0 < π m) ∧
              (∀ (n : S → ℕ),
                  (N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n₀ n →
                    Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (m : S → ℕ), |N.transition κ t n m - π m|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
                ∀ (μ : (S → ℕ) → ℝ),
                  (∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ μ m) →
                    (∀ (m : S → ℕ), ¬(N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n₀ m → μ m = 0) →
                      HasSum μ 1 →
                        (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), μ x * N.transition κ t x m = μ m) → μ = π

    (∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 < π m) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), π x * N.fullyOpen.transition κ t x m = π m) ∧
          (∀ (n : S → ℕ),
              Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (m : S → ℕ), |N.fullyOpen.transition κ t n m - π m|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
            ∀ (μ : (S → ℕ) → ℝ),
              (∀ (m : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ μ m) →
                HasSum μ 1 →
                  (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), μ x * N.fullyOpen.transition κ t x m = μ m) → μ = π

CRNT.JumpSystem.Pmin_eq.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [DecidableEq α] (J : CRNT.JumpSystem α) (t : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t) (a b : α) :
  J.Pmin t a b =
    (Real.exp (-(J.q a * t)) * if a = b then 1 else 0) +
      ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..t, Real.exp (-(J.q a * s)) * ∑ i, J.rate i a * J.Pmin (t - s) (J.next i a) b

CRNT.Network.transition_eq {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) {t : ℝ}
  (ht : 0 ≤ t) (n m : S → ℕ) :
  N.transition κ t n m =
    (Real.exp (-(N.exitRate κ n * t)) * if n = m then 1 else 0) +
      ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..t,
        Real.exp (-(N.exitRate κ n * s)) *
          ∑ r, N.stochasticMassActionRate κ n r * N.transition κ (t - s) (N.jumpNextCount n r) m
'CRNT.Network.transition_eq' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.hasDerivAt_transition {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (n m : S → ℕ) :
  HasDerivAt (fun τ => N.transition κ τ n m)
    (-N.exitRate κ n * N.transition κ t n m +
      ∑ r, N.stochasticMassActionRate κ n r * N.transition κ t (N.jumpNextCount n r) m)
    t

        J.Pexact k (t + s) a b = ∑ j ∈ Finset.range (k + 1), ∑ c ∈ F, J.Pexact j t a c * J.Pexact (k - j) s c b

CRNT.Network.positiveRecurrence_of_endotactic {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (d : N.LinearLyapunovData κ) (hirr : ∀ (a b : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos a b) :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (b : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ π b) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → ∀ (b : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), π x * (N.jumpSystem κ).Pmin s x b = π b) ∧
          ∃ β,
            0 < β ∧
              ∃ ρ,
                0 ≤ ρ ∧
                  ρ < 1 ∧
                    ∃ C₀,
                      ∀ (a : S → ℕ) (t : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          ∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                              |(N.jumpSystem κ).Pmin t a b - π b| * (1 + β * CRNT.Network.linearObservable d.w b) ≤
                            C₀ * (1 + CRNT.Network.linearObservable d.w a) * ρ ^ ⌊t⌋₊
'CRNT.Network.positiveRecurrence_of_endotactic' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

CRNT.Network.tier_geometricErgodic {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  (T : N.CountTier) (hirr : ∀ (a b : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos a b) :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (b : S → ℕ), 0 ≤ π b) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → ∀ (b : S → ℕ), ∑' (x : S → ℕ), π x * (N.jumpSystem κ).Pmin s x b = π b) ∧
          ∃ β,
            0 < β ∧
              ∃ ρ,
                0 ≤ ρ ∧
                  ρ < 1 ∧
                    ∃ C₀,
                      ∀ (a : S → ℕ) (t : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          ∑' (b : S → ℕ), |(N.jumpSystem κ).Pmin t a b - π b| * (1 + β * N.CountLyapunov κ T b) ≤
                            C₀ * (1 + N.CountLyapunov κ T a) * ρ ^ ⌊t⌋₊

CRNT.CountableFosterDrift.{u_1} (α : Type u_1) [Countable α] (J : CRNT.JumpSystem α) : Type u_1
'CRNT.CountableFosterDrift' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.foster_hitting_time_of_constant_drift.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [DecidableEq α] (J : CRNT.JumpSystem α) (C : Finset α)
  (V : α → ℝ) (hV : ∀ (a : α), 0 ≤ V a) (hfin : ∀ (R : ℝ), {a | V a ≤ R}.Finite) {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c)
  (hdrift : ∀ a ∉ C, J.drift V a ≤ -c) {T : ℝ} (hT : 0 ≤ T) (a : α) :
  ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, 1 - ∑ b ∈ C, (J.stopOn C).Pmin t a b ≤ V a / c ∧
    (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 < t → 1 - ∑ b ∈ C, (J.stopOn C).Pmin t a b ≤ V a / (c * t)) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑ b ∈ C, (J.stopOn C).Pmin t a b) Filter.atTop (nhds 1)
'CRNT.foster_hitting_time_of_constant_drift' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.foster_lyapunov_ergodic_of_constant_drift.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [Countable α] [DecidableEq α]
  (J : CRNT.JumpSystem α) (D : CRNT.CountableFosterDrift α J) (hq : ∀ a ∉ D.C, 1 ≤ J.q a)
  (hstoch : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (a : α), HasSum (fun b => J.Pmin t a b) 1) {a₀ : α} (E : Set α)
  (hE : ∀ a ∈ E, ∀ (i : J.ι), 0 < J.rate i a → J.next i a ∈ E) (hirr : ∀ a ∈ E, ∀ b ∈ E, J.ReachPos a b)
  (ha₀ : a₀ ∈ E) :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (b : α), 0 ≤ π b) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → ∀ (b : α), ∑' (c : α), π c * J.Pmin s c b = π b) ∧
          (∀ b ∉ E, π b = 0) ∧
            (∀ b ∈ E, 0 < π b) ∧
              (∀ a ∈ E, Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (b : α), |J.Pmin t a b - π b|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
                ∀ (μ : α → ℝ),
                  (∀ (b : α), 0 ≤ μ b) →
                    (∀ b ∉ E, μ b = 0) →
                      HasSum μ 1 → (∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → ∀ (b : α), ∑' (c : α), μ c * J.Pmin s c b = μ b) → μ = π
'CRNT.foster_lyapunov_ergodic_of_constant_drift' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.exists_invariant_of_constant_drift.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [Countable α] [DecidableEq α] (J : CRNT.JumpSystem α)
  (D : CRNT.CountableFosterDrift α J) (hq : ∀ a ∉ D.C, 1 ≤ J.q a)
  (hstoch : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ∀ (a : α), HasSum (fun b => J.Pmin t a b) 1) {a₀ : α} (E : Set α)
  (hE : ∀ a ∈ E, ∀ (i : J.ι), 0 < J.rate i a → J.next i a ∈ E) (hirr : ∀ a ∈ E, ∀ b ∈ E, J.ReachPos a b)
  (ha₀ : a₀ ∈ E) :
  ∃ π, (∀ (b : α), 0 ≤ π b) ∧ HasSum π 1 ∧ ∀ (s : ℝ), 0 ≤ s → ∀ (b : α), ∑' (c : α), π c * J.Pmin s c b = π b

CRNT.Network.drift_dist_sq_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {V R Rb : ℝ} (hV : 1 ≤ V) (hR : 0 ≤ R) (hRb : R ≤ Rb) {L : NNReal}
  (hL : LipschitzOnWith L (N.massActionVectorField κ) (Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb)) (c : S → ℕ)
  (hc : ∀ (s : S), ↑(c s) ≤ R * V) (z : CRNT.Concentration S) (hz : z ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb) :
  (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).drift (fun b => ∑ s, (↑(b s) / V - z s) ^ 2) c +
      ∑ s, 2 * (↑(c s) / V - z s) * -N.massActionVectorField κ z s ≤
    (1 + 2 * ↑(Fintype.card S) * ↑L) * ∑ s, (↑(c s) / V - z s) ^ 2 +
      (↑(Fintype.card S) * N.rateErr κ R ^ 2 + N.noiseConst κ R) / V

CRNT.Network.conservative_lln {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hwinv : N.IsPInvariant w) (hwpos : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) {M T Rx : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M)
  (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) :
  ∃ C₁ C₂,
    0 ≤ C₁ ∧
      0 ≤ C₂ ∧
        ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV : 1 ≤ V) (n : S → ℕ),
          ∑ s, w s * ↑(n s) ≤ M * V →
            ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
              ∑' (m : S → ℕ), N.transition (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯) t n m * ∑ s, (↑(m s) / V - x t s) ^ 2 ≤
                gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(n s) / V - x 0 s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) t
'CRNT.Network.conservative_lln' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.conservative_lln_tendsto {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hwinv : N.IsPInvariant w) (hwpos : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) {M T Rx : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M)
  (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) (n : ℕ → S → ℕ)
  (hn : ∀ (k : ℕ), ∑ s, w s * ↑(n k s) ≤ M * (↑k + 1))
  (hn0 : Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ s, (↑(n k s) / (↑k + 1) - x 0 s) ^ 2) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) {t : ℝ}
  (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T) :
  Filter.Tendsto
    (fun k =>
      ∑' (m : S → ℕ), N.transition (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯) t (n k) m * ∑ s, (↑(m s) / (↑k + 1) - x t s) ^ 2)
    Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
'CRNT.Network.conservative_lln_tendsto' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Examples.Dimerization.lln {t : ℝ} (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :

CRNT.Network.subconservative_lln {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hwsub : ∀ (r : N.R), ∑ s, w s * N.reactionVector r s ≤ 0) (hwpos : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) {M T Rx : ℝ}
  (hM : 0 ≤ M) (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) :
  ∃ C₁ C₂,
    0 ≤ C₁ ∧
      0 ≤ C₂ ∧

          ∑ s, w s * ↑(n s) ≤ M * V →
            ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
              ∑' (m : S → ℕ), N.transition (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯) t n m * ∑ s, (↑(m s) / V - x t s) ^ 2 ≤
                gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(n s) / V - x 0 s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) t

CRNT.Network.drift_moment_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hw : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) (hmono : N.MonoWeight w) {V : ℝ} (hV : 1 ≤ V) (c : S → ℕ) :
  (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).drift (fun b => (1 + CRNT.Network.wt w b / V) ^ 4) c ≤
    N.momConst κ w * (1 + CRNT.Network.wt w c / V) ^ 4
'CRNT.Network.drift_moment_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.drift_psiObs_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hw : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) (hmono : N.MonoWeight w) {V R A : ℝ} (hV : 1 ≤ V) (hR : 0 ≤ R) {c : S → ℕ}
  (hc : CRNT.Network.wt w c ≤ R * V) :
  (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).drift (CRNT.Network.psiObs w V A) c ≤
    2 * max (CRNT.Network.wt w c / V - A) 0 * (N.inRate κ w * (1 + CRNT.Network.wInv w * A)) +
        2 * (N.inRate κ w * CRNT.Network.wInv w) * max (CRNT.Network.wt w c / V - A) 0 ^ 2 +
      N.psiConst κ w R / V
'CRNT.Network.drift_psiObs_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.frozen_mass_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hw : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) (hmono : N.MonoWeight w) {M T R V : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hT : 0 ≤ T)
  (hR : (M + 1) * Real.exp (N.levelRate κ w * T) ≤ R) (hV : 1 ≤ V) {n : S → ℕ} (hn : CRNT.Network.wt w n ≤ M * V)
  {t : ℝ} (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T) :
  ∑ b ∈ CRNT.Network.levelSet w (R * V + N.dwMax w) with b ∉ CRNT.Network.levelSet w (R * V),
      ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).stopOutside (CRNT.Network.levelSet w (R * V))).Pmin t n b ≤
    N.psiConst κ w R * Real.exp (N.psiRate κ w * T) / V
'CRNT.Network.frozen_mass_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.frozen_dist_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {w : S → ℝ} (hw : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) (hmono : N.MonoWeight w) {M T R Rx : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hT : 0 ≤ T)
  (hR : (M + 1) * Real.exp (N.levelRate κ w * T) ≤ R) (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S)
  (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T)) (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) :
  ∃ C₁ C₂,
    0 ≤ C₁ ∧
      0 ≤ C₂ ∧
        ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV : 1 ≤ V) (n : S → ℕ),
          CRNT.Network.wt w n ≤ M * V →
            ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
              ∑ b ∈ CRNT.Network.levelSet w (R * V + N.dwMax w),
                  ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).stopOutside (CRNT.Network.levelSet w (R * V))).Pmin t n b *
                    ∑ s, (↑(b s) / V - x t s) ^ 2 ≤
                gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(n s) / V - x 0 s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) t

                      gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(n s) / V - x 0 s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) t + C₃ * (L ^ 2 / V + 1 / L ^ 2)

  {T F : ℝ} (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hF : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, ∑ s, N.massActionVectorField κ (x t) s ^ 2 ≤ F) {τ₀ σ : ℝ} (hτ₀ : 0 ≤ τ₀) (hσ : 0 ≤ σ)
  (hT : τ₀ + σ ≤ T) : ∑ s, (x (τ₀ + σ) s - x τ₀ s) ^ 2 ≤ ↑(Fintype.card S) * F * σ ^ 2
'CRNT.Network.sq_sub_le_of_field_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.exit_mass_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) {T Rx δ : ℝ}
  (hRx : 0 ≤ Rx) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) :
  ∃ C₁ C₂ F,
    1 ≤ C₁ ∧
      0 ≤ C₂ ∧
        0 ≤ F ∧
          (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, ∑ s, N.massActionVectorField κ (x t) s ^ 2 ≤ F) ∧
            ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV : 1 ≤ V) (τ₀ h : ℝ),
              0 ≤ τ₀ →
                0 ≤ h →
                  τ₀ + h ≤ T →
                    4 * ↑(Fintype.card S) * F * h ^ 2 ≤ δ ^ 2 →
                      ∀ (c : S → ℕ),
                        (∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                            if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet V Rx (x τ₀) δ then 0
                            else
                              ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).stopOutside
                                    (CRNT.Network.ballSet V Rx (x τ₀) δ)).Pmin
                                h c b) ≤
                          4 / δ ^ 2 * gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(c s) / V - x τ₀ s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) h
'CRNT.Network.exit_mass_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.kurtz_lln_uniform {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {T Rx δ : ℝ} (hT : 0 < T) (hRx : 0 ≤ Rx) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S)
  (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T)) (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) (n : ℕ → S → ℕ)
  (hn0 : Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ s, (↑(n k s) / (↑k + 1) - x 0 s) ^ 2) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  ∃ m,
    0 < m ∧
      (∀ i < m, ∀ σ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m), ∑ s, (x (↑i * (T / ↑m) + σ) s - x (↑i * (T / ↑m)) s) ^ 2 ≤ δ ^ 2 / 4) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun k =>
            ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m,
              ∑' (c : S → ℕ),
                N.transition (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯) (↑i * (T / ↑m)) (n k) c *
                  ∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                    if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ then 0
                    else
                      ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).stopOutside
                            (CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ)).Pmin
                        (T / ↑m) c b)
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
'CRNT.Network.kurtz_lln_uniform' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

                ∑' (b : Fin 1 → ℕ),

  (hx : HasFDerivAt f' f'' x) (w : E) :

  {Rb : ℝ} {xs : CRNT.Concentration S} (hxs : xs ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb) :
  ∃ L₂,
    0 ≤ L₂ ∧
      ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb,
        ‖N.massActionVectorField κ z - N.massActionVectorField κ xs -
              (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) xs) (z - xs)‖ ≤
          L₂ * ‖z - xs‖ ^ 2
'CRNT.Network.exists_taylor_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.exists_rate_lipschitz {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {Rb : ℝ} {xs : CRNT.Concentration S} (hxs : xs ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb) :
  ∃ Lρ,
    0 ≤ Lρ ∧
      ∀ (r : N.R), ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rb, |N.massActionRate κ r z - N.massActionRate κ r xs| ≤ Lρ * ‖z - xs‖
'CRNT.Network.exists_rate_lipschitz' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.generator_error_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {xs : CRNT.Concentration S} (heq : N.massActionVectorField κ xs = 0) {R₁ Θ : ℝ} (hR₁ : 0 ≤ R₁) (hΘ : 0 ≤ Θ)
  (hxsB : xs ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => R₁) :
  ∃ C V₀,
    0 ≤ C ∧
      1 ≤ V₀ ∧
        ∀ (θ θ' : S → ℝ),
          ∑ s, |θ s| ≤ Θ →
            (∀ (v : S → ℝ), ∑ s, θ' s * v s = -∑ s, θ s * (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) xs) v s) →
              ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV1 : 1 ≤ V),
                V₀ ≤ V →
                  ∀ (c : S → ℕ),
                    (∀ (s : S), ↑(c s) ≤ R₁ * V) →
                      ‖∑ r,
                                ↑(N.stochasticMassActionRate (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯) c r) *
                                  (Complex.exp (Complex.I * ↑((∑ s, θ s * N.reactionVector r s) / √V)) - 1) +
                              Complex.I * ↑(∑ s, θ' s * CRNT.Network.zeta V xs c s) +
                            ↑(N.quadG κ xs θ / 2)‖ ≤
                        C * (1 + ‖CRNT.Network.zeta V xs c‖ ^ 2) / √V
'CRNT.Network.generator_error_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.cdrift_expObs {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) {V : ℝ}
  (hV : 0 < V) (xs : CRNT.Concentration S) (θ : S → ℝ) (a : ℂ) (c : S → ℕ) :
  (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V hV)).cdrift
      (fun b => Complex.exp (Complex.I * ↑(∑ s, θ s * CRNT.Network.zeta V xs b s) - a)) c =
    Complex.exp (Complex.I * ↑(∑ s, θ s * CRNT.Network.zeta V xs c s) - a) *
      ∑ r,
        ↑(N.stochasticMassActionRate (N.volumeScale κ V hV) c r) *
          (Complex.exp (Complex.I * ↑((∑ s, θ s * N.reactionVector r s) / √V)) - 1)
'CRNT.Network.cdrift_expObs' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.clt_error {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) {w : S → ℝ}
  (hwinv : N.IsPInvariant w) (hwpos : ∀ (s : S), 0 < w s) {xs : CRNT.Concentration S}
  (heq : N.massActionVectorField κ xs = 0) (hxs0 : ∀ (s : S), 0 ≤ xs s) {M T : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hT : 0 < T)
  (θf θf' : ℝ → S → ℝ) (hθd : ∀ (u : ℝ), HasDerivAt θf (θf' u) u)
  (hadj :
    ∀ u ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
      ∀ (v : S → ℝ), ∑ s, θf' u s * v s = -∑ s, θf u s * (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) xs) v s) :
  ∃ K V₀,
    0 ≤ K ∧
      1 ≤ V₀ ∧
        ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV1 : 1 ≤ V),
          V₀ ≤ V →
            ∀ (n : S → ℕ),
              ∑ s, w s * ↑(n s) ≤ M * V →
                ‖∑' (m : S → ℕ),
                        ↑(N.transition (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯) T n m) *
                          Complex.exp (Complex.I * ↑(∑ s, θf T s * CRNT.Network.zeta V xs m s)) -
                      Complex.exp
                        (Complex.I * ↑(∑ s, θf 0 s * CRNT.Network.zeta V xs n s) -
                          ↑((∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..T, N.quadG κ xs (θf u)) / 2))‖ ≤
                  K * (1 + ∑ s, CRNT.Network.zeta V xs n s ^ 2) / √V

  (hadj :
    ∀ u ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, ∀ (v : S → ℝ), ∑ s, θf' u s * v s = -∑ s, θf u s * (fderiv ℝ (N.massActionVectorField κ) xs) v s)
  (n : ℕ → S → ℕ) (hn : ∀ (k : ℕ), ∑ s, w s * ↑(n k s) ≤ M * (↑k + 1)) {z₀ : S → ℝ}

CRNT.Network.frozen_unit_dist_le {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants)
  {T Rx : ℝ} (hRx : 0 ≤ Rx) (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) :
  ∃ C₁ C₂,
    0 ≤ C₁ ∧
      0 ≤ C₂ ∧
        ∀ (V : ℝ) (hV : 1 ≤ V) (n : S → ℕ),
          CRNT.Network.wt (fun x => 1) n ≤ (↑(Fintype.card S) * Rx + ↑(Fintype.card S) + 1) * V →
            ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
              ∑
                  b ∈
                    CRNT.Network.levelSet (fun x => 1)
                      ((↑(Fintype.card S) * Rx + ↑(Fintype.card S) + 1) * V + N.dwMax fun x => 1),
                  ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ V ⋯)).stopOutside
                          (CRNT.Network.levelSet (fun x => 1)
                            ((↑(Fintype.card S) * Rx + ↑(Fintype.card S) + 1) * V))).Pmin
                      t n b *
                    ∑ s, (↑(b s) / V - x t s) ^ 2 ≤
                gronwallBound (∑ s, (↑(n s) / V - x 0 s) ^ 2) C₁ (C₂ / V) t

CRNT.Network.fluctuation_uniform_concentration {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) {T Rx δ : ℝ} (hT : 0 < T) (hRx : 0 ≤ Rx) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1)
  (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) (n : ℕ → S → ℕ)
  (hn0 : Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ s, (↑(n k s) / (↑k + 1) - x 0 s) ^ 2) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  ∃ m,
    0 < m ∧
      (∀ i < m, ∀ σ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m), ∑ s, (x (↑i * (T / ↑m) + σ) s - x (↑i * (T / ↑m)) s) ^ 2 ≤ δ ^ 2 / 4) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, N.windowExitMass κ ⋯ Rx δ (T / ↑m) x i (n k)) Filter.atTop
            (nhds 0) ∧
          ∀ (k i : ℕ),
            i < m →
              ∀ (c : S → ℕ),
                ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m),
                  (∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                      if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ then 0
                      else (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).Pmin τ c b) ≤
                    ∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                      if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ then 0
                      else
                        ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).stopOutside
                              (CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ)).Pmin
                          (T / ↑m) c b
'CRNT.Network.fluctuation_uniform_concentration' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (hD : ∀ (i : J.ι) (a : α), J.rate i a ≠ 0 → |V (J.next i a) - V a| ≤ D) (hD0 : 0 ≤ D) (hQ : ∀ (a : α), J.q a ≤ Q)

  ∑ b ∈ F, J.Pmin T a b * ((V b - V a) / σ) ^ 2 ≤ (2 * (M / σ) * ((K + L) / σ) + (D / σ) ^ 2 * Q) * T
'CRNT.JumpSystem.fluctuation_sq_le_uniform_scaled' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Network.fluctuation_uniform_concentration_and_second_moment {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S]
  (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) {T Rx δ : ℝ} (hT : 0 < T) (hRx : 0 ≤ Rx) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1)
  (x : ℝ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (N.massActionVectorField κ (x t)) t)
  (hxB : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Set.Icc 0 fun x => Rx) (n : ℕ → S → ℕ)
  (hn0 : Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ s, (↑(n k s) / (↑k + 1) - x 0 s) ^ 2) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) (s : S) {M K L Q₀ : ℝ}
  (hM : 0 ≤ M) (hfM : ∀ (k : ℕ) (c : S → ℕ), CRNT.Network.densityObservable (↑k + 1) s c ≤ M)
  (hK :
    ∀ (k : ℕ) (c : S → ℕ),
      (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).drift (CRNT.Network.densityObservable (↑k + 1) s) c ≤ K)
  (hK0 : 0 ≤ K)
  (hL :
    ∀ (k : ℕ) (c : S → ℕ),
      -L ≤ (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).drift (CRNT.Network.densityObservable (↑k + 1) s) c)
  (hL0 : 0 ≤ L) (hQ : ∀ (k : ℕ) (c : S → ℕ), (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).q c ≤ (↑k + 1) * Q₀)
  (hQ0 : 0 ≤ Q₀) {F : Finset (S → ℕ)} {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ m,
    0 < m ∧
      (∀ i < m, ∀ σ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m), ∑ s, (x (↑i * (T / ↑m) + σ) s - x (↑i * (T / ↑m)) s) ^ 2 ≤ δ ^ 2 / 4) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun k => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, N.windowExitMass κ ⋯ Rx δ (T / ↑m) x i (n k)) Filter.atTop
            (nhds 0) ∧
          (∀ (k i : ℕ),
              i < m →
                ∀ (c : S → ℕ),
                  ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m),
                    (∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                        if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ then 0
                        else (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).Pmin τ c b) ≤
                      ∑' (b : S → ℕ),
                        if b ∈ CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ then 0
                        else
                          ((N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).stopOutside
                                (CRNT.Network.ballSet (↑k + 1) Rx (x (↑i * (T / ↑m))) δ)).Pmin
                            (T / ↑m) c b) ∧
            ∀ (k i : ℕ),
              i < m →
                ∀ (c : S → ℕ),
                  ∀ τ ∈ Set.Icc 0 (T / ↑m),
                    ∀ G ⊆ F,
                      (∀ b ∈ G,
                          ε ≤
                            |CRNT.Network.densityObservable (↑k + 1) s b -
                                CRNT.Network.densityObservable (↑k + 1) s c|) →
                        ∑ b ∈ G, (N.jumpSystem (N.volumeScale κ (↑k + 1) ⋯)).Pmin τ c b ≤
                          (2 * M * (K + L) + N.stoichBound s ^ 2 * Q₀ / (↑k + 1)) * (T / ↑m) / ε ^ 2

 Quot.sound]

  {m : MonomialOrder σ} {ι : Type u_3} [Fintype ι] {G : ι → MvPolynomial σ K} (hG0 : ∀ (i : ι), G i ≠ 0) :

  (CRNT.MixedVolume.zonotopeVolumePolynomial z fun j => if j = i then c else t j) =
    (CRNT.MixedVolume.zonotopeVolumePolynomial z fun j => if j = i then 0 else t j) +
      c *
        ((CRNT.MixedVolume.zonotopeVolumePolynomial z fun j => if j = i then 1 else t j) -
          CRNT.MixedVolume.zonotopeVolumePolynomial z fun j => if j = i then 0 else t j)

CRNT.Network.tropicalEquilibration_of_steadyStates {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (a : N.R → ℝ) (u : S → ℝ) (ε : ℕ → ℝ) (hε0 : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < ε n)
  (hε : Filter.Tendsto ε Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) (x : ℕ → CRNT.Concentration S) (hx : ∀ (n : ℕ), (x n).Positive)
  (hu : ∀ (s : S), Filter.Tendsto (fun n => Real.log (x n s) / Real.log (ε n)) Filter.atTop (nhds (u s)))
  (hss : ∀ (n : ℕ), N.IsMassActionSteadyState (N.scaledRateConstants κ a (ε n) ⋯) (x n)) (s : S)
  (hs : ∃ r, N.reactionVector r s ≠ 0) :
  ∃ r₁ r₂,
    0 < N.reactionVector r₁ s ∧
      N.reactionVector r₂ s < 0 ∧
        ∀ (r : N.R),
          N.reactionVector r s ≠ 0 →
            a r₁ + ∑ s', ↑((N.reaction r₁).source s') * u s' ≤ a r + ∑ s', ↑((N.reaction r).source s') * u s' ∧
              a r₂ + ∑ s', ↑((N.reaction r₂).source s') * u s' ≤ a r + ∑ s', ↑((N.reaction r).source s') * u s'
'CRNT.Network.tropicalEquilibration_of_steadyStates' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  {m : J → CRNT.Complex S} {c : J → E → ℝ} {h : J → ℤ} (hE : Fintype.card E = Fintype.card S)

              CRNT.Tropical.termSys m c h t (CRNT.Network.DecoratedCell.scalePoint q.w t (ψ t)) = 0 ∧
                Function.Injective

  (f : (A → ℝ) → (B → ℝ) → A → ℝ) (g : (A → ℝ) → (B → ℝ) → B → ℝ) (h : (A → ℝ) → B → ℝ)
  (Dh : (A → ℝ) → (A → ℝ) →L[ℝ] B → ℝ) {Kx : Set (A → ℝ)} {Ky : Set (B → ℝ)} {μ G ε T : ℝ} (x : ℝ → A → ℝ)

  (hzero : ∀ z ∈ Kx, g z (h z) = 0)
  (hmono : ∀ z ∈ Kx, ∀ u ∈ Ky, ∀ v ∈ Ky, ∑ b, (g z u b - g z v b) * (u b - v b) ≤ -μ * ∑ b, (u b - v b) ^ 2)
  (hG : ∀ z ∈ Kx, ∀ u ∈ Ky, ∑ b, (Dh z) (f z u) b ^ 2 ≤ G ^ 2) (hhK : ∀ z ∈ Kx, h z ∈ Ky)
  (hxc : ContinuousOn x (Set.Icc 0 T)) (hyc : ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 T))
  (hx : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt x (f (x t) (y t)) t)
  (hy : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 T, HasDerivAt y ((1 / ε) • g (x t) (y t)) t) (hxK : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, x t ∈ Kx)
  (hyK : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, y t ∈ Ky) (hhc : ContinuousOn (fun t => h (x t)) (Set.Icc 0 T)) (t : ℝ) :

    CRNT.SlowFast.fastDev h x y t ≤ Real.exp (-(μ / ε * t)) * CRNT.SlowFast.fastDev h x y 0 + (ε * G / μ) ^ 2

  {g : X → E → E} {x₀ : X} {y₀ : E} {A : E →L[ℝ] E} {μ : ℝ} (hzero : g x₀ y₀ = 0)
  (hderiv : HasFDerivAt (fun y => g x₀ y) A y₀) (hA : CRNT.HurwitzRate A μ) (hμ : 0 < μ) (y : ℝ → E)
  (hy : ∀ (t : ℝ), HasDerivAt y (g x₀ (y t)) t) :
  ∃ C,
    (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → dist (y t) y₀ ≤ C.radius) →
      (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 < t → Filter.Tendsto (fun ε => ‖y (t / ε) - y₀‖) (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 0)) ∧
        ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ‖y t - y₀‖ ≤ ‖y 0 - y₀‖ * Real.exp (-C.rate * t)

  [NormedAddCommGroup X] [NormedSpace ℝ X] [CompleteSpace X] [CompleteSpace E] {g : X → E → E} {x₀ : X} {y₀ : E}
  (hg : ContDiffAt ℝ 1 (fun p => g p.1 p.2) (x₀, y₀)) (hzero : g x₀ y₀ = 0)
  (hInv : (CRNT.fiberDeriv g x₀ y₀).IsInvertible) :

                fderiv ℝ (fun p => g p.1 p.2) (x₀, y₀) ∘SL ContinuousLinearMap.inl ℝ X E)

    ∀ z ∈ Kx,
      ∀ u ∈ Ky,
        ∀ v ∈ Ky,
          ∑ b, (N.tropicalFastField κ a w z u b - N.tropicalFastField κ a w z v b) * (u b - v b) ≤
            -μ * ∑ b, (u b - v b) ^ 2)

  (hB : 0 < d₂ * a + d₁ * d) (hdisc : 4 * d₁ * d₂ * (a * d - b * c) < (d₂ * a + d₁ * d) ^ 2) :

                b * CRNT.Turing.fourierMode μ k v₂ t x)

CRNT.Network.massActionDelayField_eq_instantaneousDelayNetwork_add_delayedProduction {S : Type} [DecidableEq S]
  [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S) (κ : N.RateConstants) (τ : N.R → ℝ) (u : CRNT.Concentration S)
  (v : N.R → CRNT.Concentration S) (hv : ∀ (r : N.R), τ r = 0 → v r = u) :
  N.massActionDelayField κ u v =
    (N.instantaneousDelayNetwork τ).massActionVectorField (N.instantaneousDelayRates τ κ) u + fun s =>
      ∑ r, κ.k r * (N.reaction r).source.massActionMonomial (v r) * ↑(if τ r = 0 then 0 else (N.reaction r).target s)

CRNT.Network.massActionDelay_local_exists_of_delayGap {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (κ : N.RateConstants) (τ : N.R → ℝ) (d : ℝ) (hd : 0 < d) (hτ : ∀ (r : N.R), 0 ≤ τ r)

CRNT.Network.gac_of_singleLinkage_decide {S : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] (N : CRNT.Network S)
  (hwr : N.WeaklyReversible) (κ : N.RateConstants) (hslc : N.computeNumLinkageClasses = 1)
  {xstar x₀ : CRNT.Concentration S} (hxs : xstar.Positive) (hcb : N.IsComplexBalanced κ xstar) (hx0 : x₀.Positive)
  (hx0compat : N.StoichCompatible x₀ xstar) (hpers : N.SingleLinkageClass → N.PersistentFrom κ x₀) :
  ∃ ϕ γ,
    (∀ (x : CRNT.Concentration S), γ x 0 = x) ∧
      (∀ (x : CRNT.Concentration S) (t : NNReal), ϕ.toFun t x = γ x ↑t) ∧
        (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → HasDerivAt (γ x₀) (N.massActionVectorField κ (γ x₀ t)) t) ∧
          omegaLimit Filter.atTop ϕ.toFun {x₀} = {xstar}

CRNT.Computation.stablyComputes_comp {k : ℕ} {α S T : Type} [DecidableEq S] [Fintype S] [DecidableEq T] [Fintype T]
  (N₁ : Fin k → CRNT.Network S) (init₁ : Fin k → α → CRNT.Computation.Config S) (o : Fin k → S) (f₁ : Fin k → α → ℕ)
  (hmono₁ : ∀ (i : Fin k) (r : (N₁ i).R), ((N₁ i).reaction r).source (o i) = 0) (w₁ : Fin k → S → ℕ)
  (hw₁ : ∀ (i : Fin k), 1 ≤ w₁ i (o i))
  (hw₁δ :
    ∀ (i : Fin k) (r : (N₁ i).R), CRNT.Computation.wsum (fun s => ↑(w₁ i s)) (CRNT.Computation.delta (N₁ i) r) ≤ -1)
  (hout₁ :
    ∀ (i : Fin k) (a : α) (c : CRNT.Computation.Config S),
      CRNT.Computation.Reach (N₁ i) (init₁ i a) c → CRNT.Computation.Terminal (N₁ i) c → c (o i) = f₁ i a)
  (N₂ : CRNT.Network T) (init₂ : (Fin k → ℕ) → CRNT.Computation.Config T) (τ : Fin k → T) (o₂ : T)
  (f₂ : (Fin k → ℕ) → ℕ) (hinit₂₁ : ∀ (v : Fin k → ℕ) (i : Fin k), init₂ v (τ i) = v i)
  (hinit₂₂ : ∀ (v : Fin k → ℕ) (t : T), (∀ (i : Fin k), t ≠ τ i) → init₂ v t = 0)
  (hτ : ∀ (i j : Fin k), τ i = τ j → i = j) (w : Fin k → ℕ) (hcoll : CRNT.Computation.Collector τ N₂ o₂ w) (w₂ : T → ℕ)
  (hw₂δ : ∀ (r : N₂.R), CRNT.Computation.wsum (fun t => ↑(w₂ t)) (CRNT.Computation.delta N₂ r) ≤ -1)
  (h₂ : CRNT.Computation.StablyComputes N₂ init₂ o₂ f₂) :
  CRNT.Computation.StablyComputes (CRNT.Computation.compNetwork N₁ o τ N₂) (fun a => CRNT.Computation.compInit init₁ a)
    (Sum.inr o₂) fun a => f₂ fun i => f₁ i a
'CRNT.Computation.stablyComputes_comp' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

CRNT.Antithetic.lyap (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) : ℝ
'CRNT.Antithetic.lyap' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.drift_lyap (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) :
  (CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.jumpSystem κ).drift (CRNT.Antithetic.lyap κ) n =
    CRNT.Antithetic.μ κ *
              (CRNT.Antithetic.phi κ (n 0 + 1) (n 1) - CRNT.Antithetic.phi κ (n 0) (n 1) -
                  CRNT.Antithetic.cc κ * ↑(n 2) +
                CRNT.Antithetic.cd κ * ↑(n 1)) +
            CRNT.Antithetic.η κ * ↑(n 0) * ↑(n 1) * (CRNT.Antithetic.cd κ * (1 - ↑(n 0) - ↑(n 1))) +
          CRNT.Antithetic.kk κ * ↑(n 0) *
            (CRNT.Antithetic.cb κ * (2 * ↑(n 2) + 1) - CRNT.Antithetic.cc κ * (↑(n 0) - ↑(n 1))) +
        CRNT.Antithetic.γ κ * ↑(n 2) *
          (CRNT.Antithetic.cb κ * (1 - 2 * ↑(n 2)) + CRNT.Antithetic.cc κ * (↑(n 0) - ↑(n 1))) +
      CRNT.Antithetic.θ κ * ↑(n 2) *
        (CRNT.Antithetic.phi κ (n 0) (n 1 + 1) - CRNT.Antithetic.phi κ (n 0) (n 1) + CRNT.Antithetic.cc κ * ↑(n 2) +
          CRNT.Antithetic.cd κ * ↑(n 0))
'CRNT.Antithetic.drift_lyap' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.drift_lyap_le (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) :
  (CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.jumpSystem κ).drift (CRNT.Antithetic.lyap κ) n ≤
    -CRNT.Antithetic.fLyap κ n + CRNT.Antithetic.cK κ
'CRNT.Antithetic.drift_lyap_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

CRNT.Antithetic.irreducible (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n m : Fin 3 → ℕ) :
  (CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.jumpSystem κ).ReachPos n m
'CRNT.Antithetic.irreducible' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop_ergodic (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), 0 < π n) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (t : ℝ),
            0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m = π m) ∧
          (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ),
              Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), |CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m - π m|)
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
            (∀ (ν : (Fin 3 → ℕ) → ℝ),
                (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), 0 ≤ ν n) →
                  HasSum ν 1 →
                    (∀ (t : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          ∀ (m : Fin 3 → ℕ),
                            ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), ν n * CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m = ν m) →
                      ν = π) ∧
              ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * CRNT.Antithetic.fLyap κ n ≤ CRNT.Antithetic.cK κ ∧
                ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * ↑(n 2) = κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rRef / κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rSense
'CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop_ergodic' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.unitRates_ergodic :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), 0 < π n) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ),
            Filter.Tendsto
              (fun t =>
                ∑' (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), |CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition CRNT.Antithetic.unitRates t n m - π m|)
              Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
          ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * ↑(n 2) = 1
'CRNT.Antithetic.unitRates_ergodic' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) : ℝ
'CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap_le_lyap (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) :
  CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap κ n ≤ CRNT.Antithetic.lyap κ n

CRNT.Antithetic.lyap_le_fLyap (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) {n : Fin 3 → ℕ} (h : n 1 ≤ n 0) :
  CRNT.Antithetic.lyap κ n ≤ CRNT.Antithetic.cM κ / CRNT.Antithetic.cε κ * CRNT.Antithetic.fLyap κ n
'CRNT.Antithetic.lyap_le_fLyap' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.drift_wLyap_neg (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) {n : Fin 3 → ℕ} (hlt : n 0 < n 1) :
  (CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.jumpSystem κ).drift (CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap κ) n =
    CRNT.Antithetic.μ κ * (CRNT.Antithetic.cd κ * (2 * ↑(n 0) + 1)) +
          CRNT.Antithetic.η κ * ↑(n 0) * ↑(n 1) * (CRNT.Antithetic.cd κ * (1 - 2 * ↑(n 0))) +
        CRNT.Antithetic.kk κ * ↑(n 0) * (CRNT.Antithetic.cb κ * (2 * ↑(n 2) + 1)) +
      CRNT.Antithetic.γ κ * ↑(n 2) * (CRNT.Antithetic.cb κ * (1 - 2 * ↑(n 2)))
'CRNT.Antithetic.drift_wLyap_neg' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.drift_wLyap_le (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) :
  ∃ c K,
    0 < c ∧
      0 ≤ K ∧
        ∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ),
          (CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.jumpSystem κ).drift (CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap κ) n ≤
            -c * CRNT.Antithetic.wLyap κ n + K
'CRNT.Antithetic.drift_wLyap_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.output_sq_moment_le (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) :
  ∃ B,
    ∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) (t : ℝ),
      0 ≤ t → ∀ (F : Finset (Fin 3 → ℕ)), ∑ m ∈ F, CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m * ↑(m 2) ^ 2 ≤ B n
'CRNT.Antithetic.output_sq_moment_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop_mean_output_tendsto (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m * ↑(m 2)) Filter.atTop
    (nhds (κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rRef / κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rSense))
'CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop_mean_output_tendsto' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
CRNT.Antithetic.unitRates_mean_output_tendsto (n : Fin 3 → ℕ) :

    (fun t => ∑' (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition CRNT.Antithetic.unitRates t n m * ↑(m 2))

CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop_ergodic_of_fosterCertificate (κ : CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.RateConstants) :
  ∃ π,
    (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), 0 < π n) ∧
      HasSum π 1 ∧
        (∀ (t : ℝ),
            0 ≤ t → ∀ (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m = π m) ∧
          (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ),
              Filter.Tendsto (fun t => ∑' (m : Fin 3 → ℕ), |CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m - π m|)
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) ∧
            (∀ (ν : (Fin 3 → ℕ) → ℝ),
                (∀ (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), 0 ≤ ν n) →
                  HasSum ν 1 →
                    (∀ (t : ℝ),
                        0 ≤ t →
                          ∀ (m : Fin 3 → ℕ),
                            ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), ν n * CRNT.Antithetic.closedLoop.transition κ t n m = ν m) →
                      ν = π) ∧
              ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * CRNT.Antithetic.fLyap κ n ≤ CRNT.Antithetic.cK κ ∧
                ∑' (n : Fin 3 → ℕ), π n * ↑(n 2) = κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rRef / κ.k CRNT.Antithetic.rSense
